ATRF2014
--------
``Geographic3D::fromSRID(Geographic3D::EPSG_ATRF2014)``

to ATRF2014 (Geographic2D)
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
.. csv-table::
    :header: "EPSG", "PHPCoord"
    :widths: 40, 60

    "| Name: geographic3D to geographic2D
    | Code: ``urn:ogc:def:coordinateOperation:EPSG::15593``
    | Extent: World",".. code-block:: php

        $point->threeDToTwoD(
            to: Geographic2D::fromSRID(Geographic2D::EPSG_ATRF2014)
        )

    "

            to: Geographic3D::fromSRID(Geographic3D::EPSG_SABA),
